Since we're building out Enki's Phase 1C Manufacturer Product Database, the goal here is to create a reliable, automated way to populate it with high-quality, structured data from vendor sites. This directly ties into Enki's core patterns: we'll essentially be scripting an "upload/parse" precursor that gathers PDFs for later drag-drop into the app, where AI can assist in extracting/spec-matching (e.g., flagging VOC mismatches for local regs or substrate risks like alkali-sensitive membranes on fresh concrete). By focusing on waterproofing-specific vendors, we enforce discipline in the database—ensuring consistency in lead times, warranties, and compatibilities to protect profit margins during submittals and change orders.
Here are some initial ideas to bounce around, structured around feasibility, efficiency, and waterproofing relevance. I'd love your thoughts on priorities or tweaks—e.g., which vendors to start with, or if we should add filters for specific product categories like fluid-applied membranes or sheet goods.
1. Vendor Selection and Prioritization

Start small: Focus on the top 5-7 you mentioned (Tremco, BASF, Carlisle, Sika, Henry) to avoid overload. We can expand later to others like Soprema, GCP (formerly Grace), Polyglass, or W.R. Meadows, which are common in waterproofing subs' workflows.
Idea: Categorize by product focus. For example, Tremco and Sika have broad sealant/membrane lines, while Carlisle excels in roofing-integrated waterproofing. This helps tailor scraping logic—e.g., search for "below-grade" or "plaza deck" keywords to filter PDFs relevant to subs (ignoring irrelevant stuff like architectural coatings).
Bounce: Should we include regional vendors (e.g., CETCO for US bentonite systems) or stick to globals? Also, how about auto-detecting "new" vendors via a seed list in the script, pulling from a CSV for easy updates?

2. Scraping Strategy: Why Selenium and What Challenges?

Selenium is spot-on for dynamic sites: Many vendor pages (e.g., tremcosealants.com/products) use JS for loading product grids, filters, or modals with PDF links. It can simulate user interactions like accepting cookies, clicking "Download PDS," or handling logins if needed (though most PDS are public).
Hybrid approach idea: Use requests/BeautifulSoup for initial page fetches where possible (faster, less resource-intensive), falling back to Selenium only for JS-heavy sites. This keeps the script lightweight—e.g., probe Tremco's site with requests first, then spin up a headless Chrome instance if needed.
Key challenges to brainstorm:
Anti-scraping: Sites like Sika might use CAPTCHA or rate limits. Idea: Add delays (random 2-5s between requests), rotate user-agents, or use proxies. For cookies, Selenium can capture and persist them via WebDriver's session.
PDF extraction: Links often end in .pdf, but sometimes they're behind forms. Idea: Use Selenium to click download buttons, then monitor a download folder (via Chrome prefs) to save files automatically.
Data relevance: Not all PDFs are PDS—some are MSDS or install guides. Idea: Filter by title/URL patterns (e.g., contains "technical data sheet" or "PDS"), and log skips for manual review.

Bounce: Have you encountered specific pain points on these sites (e.g., BASF's Master Builders portal requiring navigation through categories)? Should we add OCR fallback if PDFs are scanned images (using PyMuPDF or something light)?

3. Script Structure and Output for Enki Integration

Modular design: One main script with functions per vendor (e.g., def scrape_tremco(url): ...). Input: A config file (YAML/JSON) with vendor URLs and target categories. Output: Structured folders like /data/Tremco/Vulkem_116/PDS.pdf, plus a metadata CSV (e.g., product_name, pdf_path, scrape_date) for easy upload to Enki's backend (Supabase).
Waterproofing-specific filters: While scraping, we can extract snippets or metadata (e.g., via PDF parsing with PyPDF2) for quick wins like VOC values or warranties, but keep it optional to avoid bloat. This preps for Enki's AI assist step—e.g., flag "lead time: 4-6 weeks" to enforce procurement discipline.
Testing/Reliability: Include unit tests (e.g., mock a page with BeautifulSoup) and error handling (retry on timeouts). Run in headless mode for CI, but add verbose logging for debugging.
Bounce: For Enki's PWA flow, should the script output ZIP archives for drag-drop, or integrate directly (e.g., via API upload to Supabase)? Also, legal/ethical angle: Emphasize respectful scraping (e.g., no overload, respect robots.txt)—maybe add a compliance check?

4. Tech Stack and Setup

Python basics: Use 3.8+ for compatibility. Dependencies: selenium, webdriver-manager (auto-downloads ChromeDriver), beautifulsoup4, requests, PyPDF2 (for optional post-scrape parsing).
Installation: Guide with pip install -r requirements.txt.
Efficiency ideas: Multithreading for parallel vendor scrapes (but cautious with Selenium's resource use). Limit to 10-20 PDFs per run to test.
Bounce: Any preferred IDE (VS Code?) or env (virtualenv)? Should we containerize with Docker for reproducibility?

To address this refinement, we'll standardize Supabase client initialization and authentication handling. Currently, some pages (e.g., dashboard and home) use the useSupabase() hook from lib/supabase.ts, which leverages a lazy-initialized singleton for efficiency and consistency. Verify Existing lib/supabase.ts:

Refactor Phase 1B Sub-Pages to Use the Hook:
For each sub-page (change-order, procurement, invoice, closeout), replace createBrowserClient() with useSupabase().
Example for app_web_app_phase1b_change-order_page.tsx (apply similarly to others):tsx'use client'; // Unchanged

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from '@/components/ui/table';
import UploadZone from '@/components/forms/upload-zone';
import { parseFiles } from '@/lib/ai-parse';
import { generateExhibits } from '@/lib/ai-generate';
import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { useSupabase } from '@/lib/supabase'; // NEW: Import the hook

export default function ChangeOrder() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(null);
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [risks, setRisks] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [generatedClauses, setGeneratedClauses] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = useSupabase(); // NEW: Use hook instead of createBrowserClient

  useEffect(() => {
    const getSession = async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      setSession(session);
      if (!session) router.push('/');
    };
    getSession();

    const { data: authListener } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => setSession(session));
    return () => authListener.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, [router, supabase]);

  const handleUpload = async (acceptedFiles: File[]) => {
    setFiles(acceptedFiles);
    setUploading(true);
    setError(null);
    setRisks([]);
    setGeneratedClauses([]);

    try {
      const uploadedFiles = [];
      for (const file of acceptedFiles) {
        const { data, error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('submittals')
          .upload(`user_${session?.user.id}/${file.name}`, file);
        if (uploadError) throw uploadError;
        uploadedFiles.push(file.name);
      }
      console.log('Upload successful:', uploadedFiles);

      const parsedRisks = await parseFiles(acceptedFiles);
      console.log('Parsed risks:', parsedRisks);
      setRisks(parsedRisks);

      const clauses = await generateExhibits(parsedRisks);
      console.log('Generated clauses:', clauses);
      setGeneratedClauses(clauses);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Upload failed:', (err as Error).message);
      setError('Upload or processing failed: ' + (err as Error).message);
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!session) return null;

  // Rest of the component unchanged...
}
Repeat for procurement/page.tsx, invoice/page.tsx, and closeout/page.tsx: Replace the client creation line with const supabase = useSupabase();.


Add Global Auth Wrapper:
Create or update app/layout.tsx (or _app.tsx if using pages router—your uploads suggest app router). This wraps all pages with session management.tsx'use client'; // For hooks

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useSupabase } from '@/lib/supabase'; // Use singleton hook
import '@/app/globals.css'; // Assuming your CSS import

export default function RootLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = useSupabase();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!supabase) {
      setLoading(false);
      router.push('/'); // Redirect if no client (env issue)
      return;
    }

    const getSession = async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      setSession(session);
      setLoading(false);
      if (!session && !window.location.pathname.startsWith('/phase1b')) { // Allow guest for submittals hub if desired
        router.push('/');
      }
    };
    getSession();

    const { data: authListener } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => {
      setSession(session);
      if (!session && !window.location.pathname.startsWith('/phase1b')) {
        router.push('/');
      }
    });
    return () => authListener.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, [supabase, router]);

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center">Loading...</div>; // Simple loader
  }

  return (
    <html lang="en">
      <body>{children}</body>
    </html>
  );
}
This centralizes auth, reducing duplication in per-page useEffects. Adjust protected paths as needed (e.g., allow guest access to /phase1b per your home page links).

export default function RootLayout({
  children,

  children: React.ReactNode;
}>) {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = useSupabase();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!supabase) {
      setLoading(false);
      router.push('/'); // Redirect if no client (e.g., env issue)
      return;
    }

    const getSession = async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      setSession(session);
      setLoading(false);

Copy-paste the following code into this new file. It's essentially the client logic from my earlier suggestion, now isolated as a reusable component to avoid the error.typescriptreact'use client'; // This makes it a client component for hooks

import { useEffect, useState, ReactNode } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useSupabase } from '@/lib/supabase'; // Adjust path if needed (singleton hook)

interface AuthWrapperProps {
  children: ReactNode;
}

export default function AuthWrapper({ children }: AuthWrapperProps) {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = useSupabase();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!supabase) {
      setLoading(false);
      router.push('/'); // Redirect if no client (e.g., env issue)
      return;
    }

    const getSession = async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      setSession(session);
      setLoading(false);
      if (!session && !window.location.pathname.startsWith('/phase1b')) { // Allow guest for submittals hub; customize protected paths
        router.push('/');
      }
    };
    getSession();

    const { data: authListener } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => {
      setSession(session);
      if (!session && !window.location.pathname.startsWith('/phase1b')) {
        router.push('/');
      }
    });
    return () => authListener.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, [supabase, router]);

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center">Loading...</div>; // Customize loader (e.g., add shadcn/ui spinner)
  }

  return <>{children}</>; // Render children once auth is resolved
}
Notes:
This component handles global session checks, redirects, and loading states. It allows guest access to /phase1b (per your home page links for "Begin Submittals as Guest"), but you can adjust the path conditions (e.g., add more unprotected routes like /).
TypeScript: Uses ReactNode for children—aligns with your existing TS setup (e.g., tsconfig.json).
Waterproofing tie-in: No direct changes to PM flows, but this ensures auth consistency for user-specific storage (e.g., 'submittals' bucket with RLS for invoice PDFs).

Full updated code:typescriptreact'use client'; // Client component for hooks

import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import { useDropzone } from 'react-dropzone';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';

interface UploadZoneProps {
  onUpload: (files: File[]) => void; // Standardized prop: Callback for accepted files (TypeScript validation)
}

export default function UploadZone({ onUpload }: UploadZoneProps) {
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const onDrop = useCallback((acceptedFiles: File[]) => {
    if (typeof onUpload !== 'function') {
      setError('Upload handler not provided');
      return;
    }
    setFiles(acceptedFiles);
    setError(null);
    onUpload(acceptedFiles); // Call standardized prop
  }, [onUpload]);

  const { getRootProps, getInputProps, isDragActive } = useDropzone({
    onDrop,
    accept: { 'application/pdf': ['.pdf'] }, // PDF only for specs/subcontracts/invoices
    multiple: true,
  });

  const clearFiles = () => setFiles([]);

  return (
    <div>
      <div
        {...getRootProps()}
        className={`border-2 border-dashed p-6 rounded-md text-center cursor-pointer ${isDragActive ? 'border-blue-500 bg-blue-50' : 'border-gray-300'}`}
      >
        <input {...getInputProps()} />
        <p>{isDragActive ? 'Drop PDFs here...' : 'Drag & drop PDFs, or click to select'}</p>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <Alert variant="destructive" className="mt-4">
          <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
          <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
        </Alert>
      )}

      {files.length > 0 && (
        <div className="mt-4">
          <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold">Selected Files:</h4>
          <ul className="list-disc pl-5">
            {files.map((file) => (
              <li key={file.name} className="text-sm">{file.name}</li>
            ))}
          </ul>
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="mt-2" onClick={clearFiles}>Clear</Button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import UploadZone from "@/components/forms/upload-zone";
import { parseFiles } from "@/lib/ai-parse";
import { generateExhibits } from "@/lib/ai-generate";
import { useSupabase } from "@/lib/supabase";

export default function Phase1A() {
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [risks, setRisks] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [exhibits, setExhibits] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const supabase = useSupabase();

  const handleUpload = async (uploadedFiles: File[]) => {
    setFiles(uploadedFiles);
    setError(null);

    let parsedRisks: string[] = [];
    try {
      // Check session and role
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      if (!session) throw new Error("No session - sign in required.");
      const { data: user } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      console.log('User role:', user.user?.role); // Should be 'authenticated'

      if (user.user?.role !== 'authenticated') throw new Error("Role not authenticated - re-login or check Supabase auth.");

      // Upload
      const uploadPromises = uploadedFiles.map(async (file) => {
        const { data, error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('jobs')
          .upload(`uploads/${file.name}`, file, { upsert: true });

        if (uploadError) {
          console.error('Upload error details:', uploadError.name, uploadError.message, uploadError.statusCode);
          throw uploadError;
        }
        return data;
      });

      await Promise.all(uploadPromises);
      console.log('Upload successful');
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error("Upload/Parse Error:", err);
      setError(err.message || "Failed to upload - likely RLS policy violation. Check role, policies, or re-login. Stubbing parse.");
    } finally {
      parsedRisks = parseFiles(uploadedFiles);
      setRisks(parsedRisks);
      const generatedExhibits = generateExhibits(parsedRisks);
      setExhibits(generatedExhibits);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col items-center justify-center p-24">
      <Card className="w-[600px]">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Job Setup Wizard</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>Upload specs/subcontracts PDFs for risk parsing and exhibit generation.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-4">
          <UploadZone onUpload={handleUpload} />
          {files.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Uploaded Files:</h3>
              <ul className="list-disc pl-5">
                {files.map((file, idx) => (
                  <li key={idx}>{file.name}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}
          {risks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Detected Risks:</h3>
              {risks.map((risk, idx) => (
                <Alert key={idx} variant="warning">
                  <AlertDescription>{risk}</AlertDescription>
                </Alert>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          {exhibits.length > 0 && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Generated Exhibits:</h3>
              {exhibits.map((exhibit, idx) => (
                <Alert key={idx} variant="default">
                  <AlertDescription>{exhibit}</AlertDescription>
                </Alert>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive">
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}
          <Button onClick={() => setFiles([])} variant="outline">Clear Files</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );

Let's pay special attention to policies.Step 3: Bucket Organization in Supabase
With Step 2 confirmed (standardized onUpload prop across UploadZone usages, ensuring consistent drag-drop patterns for PDF uploads in Phase 1's PM lifecycle, like specs for risk parsing in job setup), we proceed to Step 3. This refinement addresses the noted inconsistency in Supabase storage buckets: 'jobs' for Phase 1A (e.g., subcontract uploads for exhibit generation) vs. 'submittals' for Phase 1B (e.g., vendor quotes for procurement risks like lead times on waterproofing materials). We'll standardize to a unified bucket structure for scalability, while documenting and emphasizing RLS policies to enforce user-specific access—critical for profit protection (e.g., preventing unauthorized views of sensitive invoice data with rate inconsistencies). This aligns with Enki's patterns: secure storage → parse → AI assist → generate/review, reducing risks like compliance violations in closeout warranties.
Special attention to policies: As requested, we'll dive deep into Supabase RLS setup. RLS ensures row-level security on buckets/objects, tying access to authenticated users (e.g., via auth.uid()). We'll configure policies to allow only the uploading user (or admins) to read/write their paths, preventing cross-user leaks (e.g., one PM seeing another's change order risks). This is enforced server-side, so even if client code slips, data stays protected. We'll include SQL for policies, testing for violations, and error handling tweaks to guide users (e.g., "Re-login for RLS access").
Goals for this step:

Standardize to a single 'enki-storage' bucket with sub-paths (e.g., 'jobs/user_{id}/specs.pdf' for 1A, 'submittals/user_{id}/invoices.pdf' for 1B)—easier management, no bucket sprawl.
Document bucket usage in code/comments for PM discipline (e.g., tie to lifecycle phases).
Enhance RLS policies: Create/enforce user-specific rules, with explicit error messaging in code.
Minimal code changes: Update upload paths in handleUpload functions; no UI alterations.

Implementation Steps:

Supabase Dashboard Setup (Policies Focus):
Log into your Supabase project dashboard (using NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL from .env.local).
Create a new bucket named 'enki-storage' (or rename 'jobs'/'submittals' if data exists—migrate files via SQL or manually).
Enable RLS on the bucket: In Storage > Policies, add these SQL policies (replace with your project ref if needed). These ensure:
Authenticated users can only access their own paths (e.g., prefixed with user_{auth.uid()}/).
No public access; reads/writes tied to session.
Policies are bucket-wide but path-filtered for fine-grained control.
Insert Policy (for uploads):SQL-- Name: Allow authenticated users to insert into their own path
create policy "User insert own path"
on storage.objects for insert
with check (
  bucket_id = 'enki-storage'
  and auth.role() = 'authenticated'
  and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = concat('user_', auth.uid())
);Select Policy (for reads/downloads):SQL-- Name: Allow authenticated users to read their own path
create policy "User select own path"
on storage.objects for select
using (
  bucket_id = 'enki-storage'
  and auth.role() = 'authenticated'
  and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = concat('user_', auth.uid())
);Update/Delete Policies (optional for Phase 1, but add for completeness):SQL-- Name: Allow authenticated users to update their own path
create policy "User update own path"
on storage.objects for update
with check (
  bucket_id = 'enki-storage'
  and auth.role() = 'authenticated'
  and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = concat('user_', auth.uid())
);

-- Name: Allow authenticated users to delete their own path
create policy "User delete own path"
on storage.objects for delete
using (
  bucket_id = 'enki-storage'
  and auth.role() = 'authenticated'
  and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = concat('user_', auth.uid())
);
Test Policies in Dashboard: Use the SQL editor to simulate inserts/selects as different users—should fail if path doesn't match uid.
Notes: storage.foldername(name) extracts the first folder (e.g., 'user_{id}' from 'user_{id}/file.pdf'). For admins, add a separate policy if needed (e.g., using auth.role() = 'service_role').

Is this OK to leave as is?Yes, it's perfectly OK—and in fact recommended—to leave the "Deny anonymous access" and "Deny anon inserts" policies as is under "Other policies under storage.objects" in your Supabase schema. These are standard security best practices that complement the RLS policies we're adding for the 'enki-storage' bucket:

Why They're Beneficial: These explicit deny policies act as a safety net, ensuring no unauthenticated (anon) users can read or insert objects across any buckets. This aligns with Enki's Phase 1 emphasis on profit protection—e.g., preventing leaks of sensitive PM data like subcontract PDFs with rate details or invoice reviews flagging unapproved extras. Supabase's default setup often includes similar guards, but custom ones provide an extra layer against misconfigurations.
Compatibility with Our RLS Setup: The policies I suggested in Step 3 (e.g., "User insert own path" checking auth.role() = 'authenticated') already restrict access to authenticated users only, so they don't conflict. If an anon request slips through (e.g., due to a client bug), these deny policies will block it with a 403 error, triggering our refined error messages in code (e.g., "Re-login for RLS access").
When to Review/Adjust: If you notice over-restrictive behavior during testing (e.g., legitimate authenticated uploads failing), double-check the policy conditions in the dashboard—ensure they don't override our path-based rules (e.g., via storage.foldername(name)). For Phase 1's guest access (e.g., /phase1b hub via AuthWrapper), these won't impact since guests aren't uploading. In future phases (e.g., 1C product DB sharing), we can add read-only public policies if needed, but for now, keep them to maintain tight security on waterproofing-specific files (e.g., specs with VOC compliance risks).

1. Updated apps_web_app_phase1a_page.tsx (Phase 1A: Job Setup Wizard)
This handles uploads for specs/subcontracts, parsing risks like VOC compliance, and generating exhibits for contract protection.
typescriptreact'use client';

import { useState } from "react";
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import UploadZone from "@/components/forms/upload-zone";
import { parseFiles } from "@/lib/ai-parse";
import { generateExhibits } from "@/lib/ai-generate";
import { useSupabase } from "@/lib/supabase";

export default function Phase1A() {
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [risks, setRisks] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [exhibits, setExhibits] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const supabase = useSupabase();

  const handleUpload = async (uploadedFiles: File[]) => {
    setFiles(uploadedFiles);
    setError(null);

    let parsedRisks: string[] = [];
    try {
      // Check session and role
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      if (!session) throw new Error("No session - sign in required.");
      const { data: user } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      console.log('User role:', user.user?.role); // Should be 'authenticated'

      if (user.user?.role !== 'authenticated') throw new Error("Role not authenticated - re-login or check Supabase auth.");

      // Standardized bucket: 'enki-storage' with phase sub-paths (e.g., 'jobs/' for setup, 'submittals/' for reviews).
      // RLS enforces user_${id}/ prefix for security—errors indicate auth/policy issues.
      const uploadPromises = uploadedFiles.map(async (file) => {
        const { data, error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('enki-storage')
          .upload(`jobs/user_${session?.user.id}/${file.name}`, file, { upsert: true });

        if (uploadError) {
          console.error('Upload error details:', uploadError.name, uploadError.message, uploadError.statusCode);
          throw uploadError;
        }
        return data;
      });

      await Promise.all(uploadPromises);
      console.log('Upload successful');
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error("Upload/Parse Error:", err);
      setError(err.message || "Upload failed - check RLS policies (e.g., authenticated role, user path match) or re-login. Stubbing parse.");
    } finally {
      parsedRisks = parseFiles(uploadedFiles);
      setRisks(parsedRisks);
      const generatedExhibits = generateExhibits(parsedRisks);
      setExhibits(generatedExhibits);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col items-center justify-center p-24">
      <Card className="w-[600px]">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Job Setup Wizard</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>Upload specs/subcontracts PDFs for risk parsing and exhibit generation.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-4">
          <UploadZone onUpload={handleUpload} />
          {files.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Uploaded Files:</h3>
              <ul className="list-disc pl-5">
                {files.map((file, idx) => (
                  <li key={idx}>{file.name}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}
          {risks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Detected Risks:</h3>
              {risks.map((risk, idx) => (
                <Alert key={idx} variant="warning">
                  <AlertDescription>{risk}</AlertDescription>
                </Alert>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          {exhibits.length > 0 && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Generated Exhibits:</h3>
              {exhibits.map((exhibit, idx) => (
                <Alert key={idx} variant="default">
                  <AlertDescription>{exhibit}</AlertDescription>
                </Alert>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive">
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}
          <Button onClick={() => setFiles([])} variant="outline">Clear Files</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}
2. Updated apps_web_app_phase1b_procurement_page.tsx (Phase 1B: Procurement Review)
This handles uploads for quotes/vendor data, focusing on stock/lead time risks for waterproofing materials.
typescriptreact'use client'; // Client-side for hooks/interactivity

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useSupabase } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from '@/components/ui/table';
import UploadZone from '@/components/forms/upload-zone'; // Adjust if named export
import { parseFiles } from '@/lib/ai-parse';
import { generateSubmittals } from '@/lib/ai-generate';
import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';

export default function Procurement() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(null);
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [risks, setRisks] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [generatedClauses, setGeneratedClauses] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = useSupabase();

  useEffect(() => {
    const getSession = async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      setSession(session);
      if (!session) router.push('/'); // Redirect guests
    };
    getSession();

    const { data: authListener } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => setSession(session));
    return () => authListener.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, [router, supabase]);

  const handleUpload = async (acceptedFiles: File[]) => {
    setFiles(acceptedFiles);
    setUploading(true);
    setError(null);
    setRisks([]);
    setGeneratedClauses([]);

    try {
      // Standardized bucket: 'enki-storage' with phase sub-paths (e.g., 'jobs/' for setup, 'submittals/' for reviews).
      // RLS enforces user_${id}/ prefix for security—errors indicate auth/policy issues.
      for (const file of acceptedFiles) {
        const { error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('enki-storage')
          .upload(`submittals/user_${session?.user.id}/${file.name}`, file);
        if (uploadError) throw uploadError;
      }

      // Stub AI parse for risks (waterproofing-specific)
      const parsedRisks = await parseFiles(acceptedFiles);
      setRisks(parsedRisks);

      // Stub generate clauses/POs using ai-generate.ts
      const clauses = await generateSubmittals(parsedRisks);
      setGeneratedClauses(clauses);
    } catch (err) {
      setError('Upload or processing failed: ' + (err as Error).message || "Upload failed - check RLS policies (e.g., authenticated role, user path match) or re-login.");
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!session) return null; // Loading during redirect

  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto p-4">
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Phase 1B: Procurement Review</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <p className="mb-4">Upload PDFs (quotes, vendor data, specs) for AI review. Focus: Stock issues, lead times, rate consistency for waterproofing materials.</p>
          <UploadZone onUpload={handleUpload} /> {/* Drag-drop upload here */}

          {uploading && <p className="mt-4">Uploading and processing...</p>}

          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive" className="mt-4">
              <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
              <AlertTitle>Error</AlertTitle>
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}

          {risks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Identified Risks</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Risk Description</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {risks.map((risk, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{risk}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
            </div>
          )}

          {generatedClauses.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Generated PO Clauses</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Clause</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {generatedClauses.map((clause, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{clause}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
              <Button className="mt-4" onClick={() => alert('Stub: Email protected POs')}>One-Click Email POs</Button>
            </div>
          )}

          <Button className="mt-6" variant="outline" onClick={() => router.push('/phase1b')}>Back to Submittals Wizard</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}
3. Updated apps_web_app_phase1b_invoice_page.tsx (Phase 1B: Invoice Review)
This handles uploads for vendor invoices/pay apps, focusing on rate consistency and unapproved extras for waterproofing billing.
typescriptreact'use client'; // Client component for hooks and interactivity

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useSupabase } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from '@/components/ui/table';
import UploadZone from '@/components/forms/upload-zone';
import { parseFiles } from '@/lib/ai-parse'; // Stub for risks (billing-specific)
import { generateExhibits } from '@/lib/ai-generate'; // Stub for review notes/clauses (or use generateSubmittals)
import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';

export default function InvoiceReview() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(null);
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [risks, setRisks] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [generatedNotes, setGeneratedNotes] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = useSupabase();

  useEffect(() => {
    const getSession = async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      setSession(session);
      if (!session) router.push('/');
    };
    getSession();

    const { data: authListener } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => setSession(session));
    return () => authListener.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, [router, supabase]);

  const handleUpload = async (acceptedFiles: File[]) => {
    setFiles(acceptedFiles);
    setUploading(true);
    setError(null);
    setRisks([]);
    setGeneratedNotes([]);

    try {
      // Standardized bucket: 'enki-storage' with phase sub-paths (e.g., 'jobs/' for setup, 'submittals/' for reviews).
      // RLS enforces user_${id}/ prefix for security—errors indicate auth/policy issues.
      const uploadedFiles = [];
      for (const file of acceptedFiles) {
        const { data, error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('enki-storage')
          .upload(`submittals/user_${session?.user.id}/${file.name}`, file);
        if (uploadError) throw uploadError;
        uploadedFiles.push(file.name);
      }
      console.log('Upload successful:', uploadedFiles); // Log for debug

      const parsedRisks = await parseFiles(acceptedFiles); // Mock: 'Risk: Rate mismatch on membrane install'
      console.log('Parsed risks:', parsedRisks);
      setRisks(parsedRisks);

      const notes = await generateExhibits(parsedRisks); // Mock: 'Note: Verify rates per Exhibit A'
      console.log('Generated notes:', notes);
      setGeneratedNotes(notes);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Upload failed:', (err as Error).message);
      setError('Upload or processing failed: ' + (err as Error).message || "Upload failed - check RLS policies (e.g., authenticated role, user path match) or re-login.");
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!session) return null;

  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto p-4">
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Phase 1B: Invoice Review</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <p className="mb-4">Upload PDFs (vendor invoices, pay apps) for AI review. Focus: Rate consistency, unapproved extras, compliance billing for waterproofing materials.</p>
          <UploadZone onUpload={handleUpload} />

          {uploading && <p className="mt-4">Uploading and processing...</p>}

          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive" className="mt-4">
              <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
              <AlertTitle>Error</AlertTitle>
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}

          {risks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Identified Risks</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Risk Description</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {risks.map((risk, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{risk}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
            </div>
          )}

          {generatedNotes.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Generated Review Notes</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Note/Clause</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {generatedNotes.map((note, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{note}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
              <Button className="mt-4" onClick={() => alert('Stub: Email invoice review notes')}>One-Click Email Review</Button>
            </div>
          )}

          <Button className="mt-6" variant="outline" onClick={() => router.push('/phase1b')}>Back to Submittals Wizard</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function Closeout() {

      setRisks(parsedRisks);

      console.log('Generated package:', packageItems);
      setGeneratedPackage(packageItems);

typescriptreact'use client'; // Client component for hooks and interactivity

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useSupabase } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from '@/components/ui/table';
import UploadZone from '@/components/forms/upload-zone';
import { parseFiles } from '@/lib/ai-parse'; // Stub for risks
import { generateExhibits } from '@/lib/ai-generate'; // Stub for clauses (or use generateSubmittals)
import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';

export default function ChangeOrder() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(null);
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [risks, setRisks] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [generatedClauses, setGeneratedClauses] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = useSupabase();

  useEffect(() => {
    const getSession = async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      setSession(session);
      if (!session) router.push('/');
    };
    getSession();

    const { data: authListener } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => setSession(session));
    return () => authListener.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, [router, supabase]);

  const handleUpload = async (acceptedFiles: File[]) => {
    setFiles(acceptedFiles);
    setUploading(true);
    setError(null);
    setRisks([]);
    setGeneratedClauses([]);

    try {
      // Standardized bucket: 'enki-storage' with phase sub-paths (e.g., 'jobs/' for setup, 'submittals/' for reviews).
      // RLS enforces user_${id}/ prefix for security—errors indicate auth/policy issues.
      const uploadedFiles = [];

        const { data, error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('enki-storage')
          .upload(`submittals/user_${session?.user.id}/${file.name}`, file);
        if (uploadError) throw uploadError;
        uploadedFiles.push(file.name); // Track for logging
      }
      console.log('Upload successful:', uploadedFiles); // Log success with file names

      // Stub AI parse for risks (waterproofing-specific, e.g., scope changes)
      const parsedRisks = await parseFiles(acceptedFiles);
      console.log('Parsed risks:', parsedRisks); // Log risks for debug
      setRisks(parsedRisks);

      // Stub generate clauses using ai-generate.ts
      const clauses = await generateExhibits(parsedRisks);
      console.log('Generated clauses:', clauses); // Log for debug
      setGeneratedClauses(clauses);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Upload failed:', (err as Error).message);
      setError('Upload or processing failed: ' + (err as Error).message || "Upload failed - check RLS policies (e.g., authenticated role, user path match) or re-login.");
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!session) return null;

  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto p-4">
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Phase 1B: Change Order Review</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <p className="mb-4">Upload PDFs (change directives, RFIs) for AI review. Focus: Scope risks, pricing protection, sequencing impacts for waterproofing.</p>
          <UploadZone onUpload={handleUpload} />

          {uploading && <p className="mt-4">Uploading and processing...</p>}

          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive" className="mt-4">
              <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
              <AlertTitle>Error</AlertTitle>
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}

          {risks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Identified Risks</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Risk Description</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {risks.map((risk, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{risk}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
            </div>
          )}

          {generatedClauses.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Generated Change Order Clauses</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>

                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>

            </div>
          )}

          <Button className="mt-6" variant="outline" onClick={() => router.push('/phase1b')}>Back to Submittals Wizard</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );

(anonymous) @ dev-backend-dom.ts:126The error you're seeing ("StorageApiError: new row violates row-level security policy" with a 400/403 status) is a classic Supabase RLS violation during the upload insert. Based on the console logs:

The request is to /storage/v1/object/enki-storage/jobs/user_a288c013-35e9-4d16-8313-31804fae9b9b/Katelyn%20-%20Federal%202024%20Taxes.pdf (correct unified bucket and path per Step 3).
The role is 'authenticated' (good, from console: 'User role: authenticated').
But the policy is rejecting because the check doesn't match the path structure.

Root Cause
The RLS policies I suggested earlier check the first folder ([1]) for 'user_{uid}', but your path is 'jobs/user_{uid}/file.pdf' (or 'submittals/user_{uid}/file.pdf' for 1B). So:

storage.foldername(name) = ['jobs', 'user_{uid}'] (or ['submittals', 'user_{uid}']).
[1] = 'jobs' (or 'submittals'), [2] = 'user_{uid}'.
The policy is failing because it expects [1] == 'user_{uid}', not [2].

This is my mistake in the index—Supabase arrays start at 1, and we need to check the second level for the user prefix while allowing phase folders (jobs/submittals) at the first level.
Quick Fix: Update RLS Policies in Supabase Dashboard
Go to your Supabase dashboard > Storage > Policies (for 'enki-storage' bucket). Delete the existing custom policies from Step 3, then add these updated ones. They now:

Check [1] IN ('jobs', 'submittals') for phase organization.
Check [2] = 'user_' || auth.uid() for user-specific security.
Keep 'authenticated' role requirement.
Your existing "Deny anonymous access" and "Deny anon inserts" policies are fine and won't conflict—they add extra anon guards.

Updated SQL Policies (copy-paste into dashboard "New Policy" forms, one per policy):
Insert Policy (for uploads):
SQL-- Name: Allow authenticated users to insert into their own phase/user path
create policy "User insert own phase/user path"
on storage.objects for insert
with check (
  bucket_id = 'enki-storage'
  and auth.role() = 'authenticated'
  and (storage.foldername(name))[1] IN ('jobs', 'submittals')
  and (storage.foldername(name))[2] = concat('user_', auth.uid())
);
Select Policy (for reads/downloads):
SQL-- Name: Allow authenticated users to read their own phase/user path
create policy "User select own phase/user path"
on storage.objects for select
using (
  bucket_id = 'enki-storage'
  and auth.role() = 'authenticated'
  and (storage.foldername(name))[1] IN ('jobs', 'submittals')
  and (storage.foldername(name))[2] = concat('user_', auth.uid())
);
Update Policy (optional, but recommended for upsert):
SQL-- Name: Allow authenticated users to update their own phase/user path
create policy "User update own phase/user path"
on storage.objects for update
with check (
  bucket_id = 'enki-storage'
  and auth.role() = 'authenticated'
  and (storage.foldername(name))[1] IN ('jobs', 'submittals')
  and (storage.foldername(name))[2] = concat('user_', auth.uid())
);
Delete Policy (optional for cleanup):
SQL-- Name: Allow authenticated users to delete their own phase/user path
create policy "User delete own phase/user path"
on storage.objects for delete
using (
  bucket_id = 'enki-storage'
  and auth.role() = 'authenticated'
  and (storage.foldername(name))[1] IN ('jobs', 'submittals')
  and (storage.foldername(name))[2] = concat('user_', auth.uid())
);

After adding, enable them if not auto-enabled.
Test in Dashboard: Use the SQL editor to insert a mock object with path 'jobs/user_{your_uid}/test.txt'—should succeed as authenticated, fail otherwise.

Code Remains As-Is (No Changes Needed)
Your code updates from earlier are correct—the path jobs/user_${session?.user.id}/${file.name} matches the updated policies. The error was policy-side, not code.

Step 4: Enhance AI Stubs in lib/ai-generate.ts and lib/ai-parse.ts
This refinement consolidates the stubbed AI logic for better maintainability and Phase 1 scalability. Currently, ai-generate.ts has separate functions (generateExhibits, generateSubmittals) with overlapping mocks—e.g., generating clauses from risks like "Lead time >4wks" for POs or "Rate mismatch" for invoice notes. Update lib/ai-parse.ts (Enhance Parsing Stubs):
Make parseFiles configurable with focus param for phase-specific risks.
Full updated code (copy-paste to replace):TypeScriptexport const parseFiles = (files: File[], options: { focus?: 'setup' | 'procurement' | 'invoice' | 'closeout' | 'change-order' } = {}) => {
  // Stub for AI parsing: Mock risks from uploaded PDFs (configurable Claude/Grok later)
  // Ignore actual file contents for dev testing; return waterproofing-specific mocks based on focus
  const { focus } = options;
  let mocks: string[] = [];

  switch (focus) {
    case 'setup':
      mocks = [
        "Risk: Substrate mismatch (e.g., incompatible membrane on gypsum)",
        "Risk: VOC compliance violation in adhesive specs for CA jurisdiction"
      ];
      break;
    case 'procurement':
      mocks = [
        "Risk: Lead time for flashing materials exceeds GC schedule",
        "Risk: Stock issues on below-grade membranes"
      ];
      break;
    case 'invoice':
      mocks = [
        "Risk: Rate mismatch on membrane install",
        "Risk: Unapproved extras in billing"
      ];
      break;
    case 'closeout':
      mocks = [
        "Risk: Incomplete warranty on below-grade waterproofing",
        "Risk: Punch list resolution pending for sequencing issues"
      ];
      break;
    case 'change-order':
      mocks = [
        "Risk: Scope change without pricing protection",
        "Risk: Sequencing dependency not addressed (e.g., prior to drywall)"
      ];
      break;
    default:
      mocks = [
        "Risk: Substrate mismatch (e.g., incompatible membrane on gypsum)",
        "Risk: Lead time for flashing materials exceeds GC schedule",
        "Risk: VOC compliance violation in adhesive specs for CA jurisdiction",
        "Risk: Sequencing dependency not addressed (e.g., prior to drywall install)"
      ];
  }

  return mocks;
};

Update lib/ai-generate.ts (Merge and Enhance Generation Stubs):
Merge into generateFromRisks with type/context params.
Full updated code (copy-paste to replace):TypeScriptexport const generateFromRisks = async (risks: string[], options: { type: 'exhibits' | 'clauses' | 'notes' | 'packages'; context?: { jurisdiction?: string; materialType?: string } } = { type: 'exhibits' }) => {
  // Stub for AI generation: Mock outputs from parsed risks (configurable Claude/Grok later)
  // Outputs for one-click emails/POs; add context for waterproofing-specific (e.g., VOC per jurisdiction)
  const { type, context = {} } = options;
  const { jurisdiction = 'US', materialType = 'membrane' } = context;

  if (!risks || risks.length === 0) return [];

  return risks.map((risk) => {
    const riskDetail = risk.split(': ')[1] || risk; // Fallback if no ': ' format
    let prefix = '';
    switch (type) {
      case 'exhibits':
        prefix = `Exhibit Clause: Require GC/vendor confirmation on ${riskDetail}`;
        break;
      case 'clauses':
        prefix = `PO Clause: Address ${riskDetail} with vendor stock confirmation`;
        break;
      case 'notes':
        prefix = `Review Note: Verify ${riskDetail} per Exhibit A`;
        break;
      case 'packages':
        prefix = `Closeout Item: Resolve ${riskDetail} with GC sign-off`;
        break;
      default:
        prefix = `Generated: Mitigate ${riskDetail}`;
    }
    // Add context (e.g., jurisdiction for VOC, material for substrate)
    if (riskDetail.includes('VOC')) prefix += ` (per ${jurisdiction} regs)`;
    if (riskDetail.includes('Substrate')) prefix += ` for ${materialType} compatibility`;

    return prefix + ' to mitigate liability.';
  });
};

// Deprecated aliases for backward compat (remove in future)
export const generateExhibits = (risks: string[]) => generateFromRisks(risks, { type: 'exhibits' });
export const generateSubmittals = (risks: string[]) => generateFromRisks(risks, { type: 'clauses' });

This incorporates the enhanced stubs: parseFiles with { focus: 'setup' } for job-specific risks (e.g., substrate/VOC), and generateFromRisks with { type: 'exhibits', context: { jurisdiction: 'CA' } } for CA-aware clauses.
typescriptreact'use client';

import { useState } from "react";
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import UploadZone from "@/components/forms/upload-zone";
import { parseFiles } from "@/lib/ai-parse";
import { generateFromRisks } from "@/lib/ai-generate";
import { useSupabase } from "@/lib/supabase";

export default function Phase1A() {
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [risks, setRisks] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [exhibits, setExhibits] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const supabase = useSupabase();

  const handleUpload = async (uploadedFiles: File[]) => {
    setFiles(uploadedFiles);
    setError(null);

    let parsedRisks: string[] = [];
    try {
      // Check session and role
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      if (!session) throw new Error("No session - sign in required.");
      const { data: user } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      console.log('User role:', user.user?.role); // Should be 'authenticated'

      if (user.user?.role !== 'authenticated') throw new Error("Role not authenticated - re-login or check Supabase auth.");

      // Standardized bucket: 'enki-storage' with phase sub-paths (e.g., 'jobs/' for setup, 'submittals/' for reviews).
      // RLS enforces user_${id}/ prefix for security—errors indicate auth/policy issues.
      const uploadPromises = uploadedFiles.map(async (file) => {
        const { data, error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('enki-storage')
          .upload(`jobs/user_${session?.user.id}/${file.name}`, file, { upsert: true });

        if (uploadError) {
          console.error('Upload error details:', uploadError.name, uploadError.message, uploadError.statusCode);
          throw uploadError;
        }
        return data;
      });

      await Promise.all(uploadPromises);
      console.log('Upload successful');
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error("Upload/Parse Error:", err);
      setError(err.message || "Upload failed - check RLS policies (e.g., authenticated role, user path match) or re-login. Stubbing parse.");
    } finally {
      parsedRisks = parseFiles(uploadedFiles, { focus: 'setup' }); // Enhanced: Phase-specific risks
      setRisks(parsedRisks);
      const generatedExhibits = await generateFromRisks(parsedRisks, { type: 'exhibits', context: { jurisdiction: 'CA' } }); // Enhanced: With jurisdiction context for VOC
      setExhibits(generatedExhibits);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col items-center justify-center p-24">
      <Card className="w-[600px]">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Job Setup Wizard</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>Upload specs/subcontracts PDFs for risk parsing and exhibit generation.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-4">
          <UploadZone onUpload={handleUpload} />
          {files.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Uploaded Files:</h3>
              <ul className="list-disc pl-5">
                {files.map((file, idx) => (
                  <li key={idx}>{file.name}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}
          {risks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Detected Risks:</h3>
              {risks.map((risk, idx) => (
                <Alert key={idx} variant="warning">
                  <AlertDescription>{risk}</AlertDescription>
                </Alert>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          {exhibits.length > 0 && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Generated Exhibits:</h3>
              {exhibits.map((exhibit, idx) => (
                <Alert key={idx} variant="default">
                  <AlertDescription>{exhibit}</AlertDescription>
                </Alert>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive">
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}
          <Button onClick={() => setFiles([])} variant="outline">Clear Files</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}
Updated Phase 1B Procurement: apps_web_app_phase1b_procurement_page.tsx
This uses { focus: 'procurement' } for lead time/stock risks, and { type: 'clauses' } for PO clauses, with material context.
typescriptreact'use client'; // Client-side for hooks/interactivity

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useSupabase } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from '@/components/ui/table';
import UploadZone from '@/components/forms/upload-zone'; // Adjust if named export
import { parseFiles } from '@/lib/ai-parse';
import { generateFromRisks } from '@/lib/ai-generate';
import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';

export default function Procurement() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(null);
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [risks, setRisks] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [generatedClauses, setGeneratedClauses] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = useSupabase();

  useEffect(() => {
    const getSession = async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      setSession(session);
      if (!session) router.push('/'); // Redirect guests
    };
    getSession();

    const { data: authListener } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => setSession(session));
    return () => authListener.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, [router, supabase]);

  const handleUpload = async (acceptedFiles: File[]) => {
    setFiles(acceptedFiles);
    setUploading(true);
    setError(null);
    setRisks([]);
    setGeneratedClauses([]);

    try {
      // Standardized bucket: 'enki-storage' with phase sub-paths (e.g., 'jobs/' for setup, 'submittals/' for reviews).
      // RLS enforces user_${id}/ prefix for security—errors indicate auth/policy issues.
      for (const file of acceptedFiles) {
        const { error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('enki-storage')
          .upload(`submittals/user_${session?.user.id}/${file.name}`, file);
        if (uploadError) throw uploadError;
      }

      // Enhanced: Stub AI parse for risks (waterproofing-specific with focus)
      const parsedRisks = parseFiles(acceptedFiles, { focus: 'procurement' });
      setRisks(parsedRisks);

      // Enhanced: Stub generate clauses/POs with type and context
      const clauses = await generateFromRisks(parsedRisks, { type: 'clauses', context: { materialType: 'flashing' } });
      setGeneratedClauses(clauses);
    } catch (err) {
      setError('Upload or processing failed: ' + (err as Error).message || "Upload failed - check RLS policies (e.g., authenticated role, user path match) or re-login.");
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!session) return null; // Loading during redirect

  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto p-4">
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Phase 1B: Procurement Review</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <p className="mb-4">Upload PDFs (quotes, vendor data, specs) for AI review. Focus: Stock issues, lead times, rate consistency for waterproofing materials.</p>
          <UploadZone onUpload={handleUpload} /> {/* Drag-drop upload here */}

          {uploading && <p className="mt-4">Uploading and processing...</p>}

          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive" className="mt-4">
              <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
              <AlertTitle>Error</AlertTitle>
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}

          {risks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Identified Risks</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Risk Description</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {risks.map((risk, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{risk}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
            </div>
          )}

          {generatedClauses.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Generated PO Clauses</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Clause</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {generatedClauses.map((clause, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{clause}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
              <Button className="mt-4" onClick={() => alert('Stub: Email protected POs')}>One-Click Email POs</Button>
            </div>
          )}

          <Button className="mt-6" variant="outline" onClick={() => router.push('/phase1b')}>Back to Submittals Wizard</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );

Updated Phase 1B Invoice: apps_web_app_phase1b_invoice_page.tsx
This uses { focus: 'invoice' } for billing risks, and { type: 'notes' } for review notes, with jurisdiction context.
typescriptreact'use client'; // Client component for hooks and interactivity

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useSupabase } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from '@/components/ui/table';
import UploadZone from '@/components/forms/upload-zone';
import { parseFiles } from '@/lib/ai-parse'; // Stub for risks (billing-specific)
import { generateFromRisks } from '@/lib/ai-generate'; // Enhanced for review notes/clauses
import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';

export default function InvoiceReview() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(null);
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [risks, setRisks] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [generatedNotes, setGeneratedNotes] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = useSupabase();

  useEffect(() => {
    const getSession = async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      setSession(session);
      if (!session) router.push('/');
    };
    getSession();

    const { data: authListener } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => setSession(session));
    return () => authListener.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, [router, supabase]);

  const handleUpload = async (acceptedFiles: File[]) => {
    setFiles(acceptedFiles);
    setUploading(true);
    setError(null);
    setRisks([]);
    setGeneratedNotes([]);

    try {
      // Standardized bucket: 'enki-storage' with phase sub-paths (e.g., 'jobs/' for setup, 'submittals/' for reviews).
      // RLS enforces user_${id}/ prefix for security—errors indicate auth/policy issues.
      const uploadedFiles = [];
      for (const file of acceptedFiles) {
        const { data, error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('enki-storage')
          .upload(`submittals/user_${session?.user.id}/${file.name}`, file);
        if (uploadError) throw uploadError;
        uploadedFiles.push(file.name);
      }
      console.log('Upload successful:', uploadedFiles); // Log for debug

      // Enhanced: Stub AI parse for risks with focus
      const parsedRisks = parseFiles(acceptedFiles, { focus: 'invoice' });
      console.log('Parsed risks:', parsedRisks);
      setRisks(parsedRisks);

      // Enhanced: Stub generate notes with type and context
      const notes = await generateFromRisks(parsedRisks, { type: 'notes', context: { jurisdiction: 'CA' } });
      console.log('Generated notes:', notes);
      setGeneratedNotes(notes);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Upload failed:', (err as Error).message);
      setError('Upload or processing failed: ' + (err as Error).message || "Upload failed - check RLS policies (e.g., authenticated role, user path match) or re-login.");
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!session) return null;

  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto p-4">
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Phase 1B: Invoice Review</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <p className="mb-4">Upload PDFs (vendor invoices, pay apps) for AI review. Focus: Rate consistency, unapproved extras, compliance billing for waterproofing materials.</p>
          <UploadZone onUpload={handleUpload} />

          {uploading && <p className="mt-4">Uploading and processing...</p>}

          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive" className="mt-4">
              <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
              <AlertTitle>Error</AlertTitle>
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}

          {risks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Identified Risks</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Risk Description</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {risks.map((risk, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{risk}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
            </div>
          )}

          {generatedNotes.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Generated Review Notes</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Note/Clause</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {generatedNotes.map((note, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{note}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
              <Button className="mt-4" onClick={() => alert('Stub: Email invoice review notes')}>One-Click Email Review</Button>
            </div>
          )}

          <Button className="mt-6" variant="outline" onClick={() => router.push('/phase1b')}>Back to Submittals Wizard</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

(anonymous) @ dev-backend-dom.ts:126typescriptreact'use client'; // Client component for hooks and interactivity

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useSupabase } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from '@/components/ui/table';
import UploadZone from '@/components/forms/upload-zone';
import { parseFiles } from '@/lib/ai-parse'; // Stub for risks (billing-specific)
import { generateFromRisks } from '@/lib/ai-generate'; // Enhanced for review notes/clauses
import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';

export default function InvoiceReview() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(null);
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [risks, setRisks] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [generatedNotes, setGeneratedNotes] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = useSupabase();

  useEffect(() => {
    const getSession = async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      setSession(session);
      if (!session) router.push('/');
    };
    getSession();

    const { data: authListener } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => setSession(session));
    return () => authListener.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, [router, supabase]);

  const handleUpload = async (acceptedFiles: File[]) => {
    setFiles(acceptedFiles);
    setUploading(true);
    setError(null);
    setRisks([]);
    setGeneratedNotes([]);

    try {
      // Standardized bucket: 'enki-storage' with phase sub-paths (e.g., 'jobs/' for setup, 'submittals/' for reviews).
      // RLS enforces user_${id}/ prefix for security—errors indicate auth/policy issues.
      const uploadedFiles = [];
      for (const file of acceptedFiles) {
        // Sanitize file name to remove invalid characters (e.g., brackets, spaces to underscores)
        const safeName = file.name.replace(/[\[\]]/g, '').replace(/\s/g, '_');
        const { data, error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('enki-storage')
          .upload(`submittals/user_${session?.user.id}/${safeName}`, file);
        if (uploadError) throw uploadError;
        uploadedFiles.push(safeName);
      }
      console.log('Upload successful:', uploadedFiles); // Log for debug

      // Enhanced: Stub AI parse for risks with focus
      const parsedRisks = parseFiles(acceptedFiles, { focus: 'invoice' });
      console.log('Parsed risks:', parsedRisks);
      setRisks(parsedRisks);

      // Enhanced: Stub generate notes with type and context
      const notes = await generateFromRisks(parsedRisks, { type: 'notes', context: { jurisdiction: 'CA' } });
      console.log('Generated notes:', notes);
      setGeneratedNotes(notes);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Upload failed:', (err as Error).message);
      setError('Upload or processing failed: ' + (err as Error).message || "Upload failed - check RLS policies (e.g., authenticated role, user path match) or re-login.");
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!session) return null;

  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto p-4">
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Phase 1B: Invoice Review</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <p className="mb-4">Upload PDFs (vendor invoices, pay apps) for AI review. Focus: Rate consistency, unapproved extras, compliance billing for waterproofing materials.</p>
          <UploadZone onUpload={handleUpload} />

          {uploading && <p className="mt-4">Uploading and processing...</p>}

          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive" className="mt-4">
              <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
              <AlertTitle>Error</AlertTitle>
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}

          {risks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Identified Risks</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Risk Description</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {risks.map((risk, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{risk}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
            </div>
          )}

          {generatedNotes.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Generated Review Notes</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Note/Clause</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {generatedNotes.map((note, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{note}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
              <Button className="mt-4" onClick={() => alert('Stub: Email invoice review notes')}>One-Click Email Review</Button>
            </div>
          )}

          <Button className="mt-6" variant="outline" onClick={() => router.push('/phase1b')}>Back to Submittals Wizard</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}
