Added sanitation to handleUpload.
typescriptreact'use client'; // Client-side for hooks/interactivity

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useSupabase } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from '@/components/ui/table';
import UploadZone from '@/components/forms/upload-zone'; // Adjust if named export
import { parseFiles } from '@/lib/ai-parse';
import { generateFromRisks } from '@/lib/ai-generate';
import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';

export default function Procurement() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(null);
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [risks, setRisks] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [generatedClauses, setGeneratedClauses] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = useSupabase();

  useEffect(() => {
    const getSession = async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      setSession(session);
      if (!session) router.push('/'); // Redirect guests
    };
    getSession();

    const { data: authListener } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => setSession(session));
    return () => authListener.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, [router, supabase]);

  const handleUpload = async (acceptedFiles: File[]) => {
    setFiles(acceptedFiles);
    setUploading(true);
    setError(null);
    setRisks([]);
    setGeneratedClauses([]);

    try {
      // Standardized bucket: 'enki-storage' with phase sub-paths (e.g., 'jobs/' for setup, 'submittals/' for reviews).
      // RLS enforces user_${id}/ prefix for security—errors indicate auth/policy issues.
      for (const file of acceptedFiles) {
        // Sanitize file name to remove invalid characters (e.g., brackets, spaces to underscores)
        const safeName = file.name.replace(/[\[\]]/g, '').replace(/\s/g, '_');
        const { error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('enki-storage')
          .upload(`submittals/user_${session?.user.id}/${safeName}`, file);
        if (uploadError) throw uploadError;
      }

      // Enhanced: Stub AI parse for risks with focus
      const parsedRisks = parseFiles(acceptedFiles, { focus: 'procurement' });
      setRisks(parsedRisks);

      // Enhanced: Stub generate clauses/POs with type and context
      const clauses = await generateFromRisks(parsedRisks, { type: 'clauses', context: { materialType: 'flashing' } });
      setGeneratedClauses(clauses);
    } catch (err) {
      setError('Upload or processing failed: ' + (err as Error).message || "Upload failed - check RLS policies (e.g., authenticated role, user path match) or re-login.");
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!session) return null; // Loading during redirect

  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto p-4">
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Phase 1B: Procurement Review</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <p className="mb-4">Upload PDFs (quotes, vendor data, specs) for AI review. Focus: Stock issues, lead times, rate consistency for waterproofing materials.</p>
          <UploadZone onUpload={handleUpload} /> {/* Drag-drop upload here */}

          {uploading && <p className="mt-4">Uploading and processing...</p>}

          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive" className="mt-4">
              <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
              <AlertTitle>Error</AlertTitle>
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}

          {risks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Identified Risks</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Risk Description</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {risks.map((risk, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{risk}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
            </div>
          )}

          {generatedClauses.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Generated PO Clauses</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Clause</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {generatedClauses.map((clause, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{clause}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
              <Button className="mt-4" onClick={() => alert('Stub: Email protected POs')}>One-Click Email POs</Button>
            </div>
          )}

          <Button className="mt-6" variant="outline" onClick={() => router.push('/phase1b')}>Back to Submittals Wizard</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';

import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';

export default function Closeout() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(null);
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [risks, setRisks] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [generatedPackage, setGeneratedPackage] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = useSupabase();

  useEffect(() => {
    const getSession = async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      setSession(session);
      if (!session) router.push('/');
    };
    getSession();

    const { data: authListener } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => setSession(session));
    return () => authListener.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, [router, supabase]);

  const handleUpload = async (acceptedFiles: File[]) => {
    setFiles(acceptedFiles);
    setUploading(true);
    setError(null);
    setRisks([]);
    setGeneratedPackage([]);

    try {
      // Standardized bucket: 'enki-storage' with phase sub-paths (e.g., 'jobs/' for setup, 'submittals/' for reviews).
      // RLS enforces user_${id}/ prefix for security—errors indicate auth/policy issues.
      const uploadedFiles = [];
      for (const file of acceptedFiles) {
        // Sanitize file name to remove invalid characters (e.g., brackets, spaces to underscores)
        const safeName = file.name.replace(/[\[\]]/g, '').replace(/\s/g, '_');
        const { data, error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('enki-storage')
          .upload(`submittals/user_${session?.user.id}/${safeName}`, file);
        if (uploadError) throw uploadError;
        uploadedFiles.push(safeName);
      }
      console.log('Upload successful:', uploadedFiles); // Log for debug

      // Enhanced: Stub AI parse for risks with focus
      const parsedRisks = parseFiles(acceptedFiles, { focus: 'closeout' });
      console.log('Parsed risks:', parsedRisks);
      setRisks(parsedRisks);

      // Enhanced: Stub generate package items with type and context
      const packageItems = await generateFromRisks(parsedRisks, { type: 'packages', context: { materialType: 'below-grade' } });
      console.log('Generated package:', packageItems);
      setGeneratedPackage(packageItems);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Upload failed:', (err as Error).message);
      setError('Upload or processing failed: ' + (err as Error).message || "Upload failed - check RLS policies (e.g., authenticated role, user path match) or re-login.");
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!session) return null;

  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto p-4">
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Phase 1B: Closeout Review</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <p className="mb-4">Upload PDFs (warranties, as-builts, lien releases) for AI review. Focus: Warranty completeness, punch list resolution, final compliance for waterproofing installations.</p>
          <UploadZone onUpload={handleUpload} />

          {uploading && <p className="mt-4">Uploading and processing...</p>}

          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive" className="mt-4">
              <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
              <AlertTitle>Error</AlertTitle>
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}

          {risks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Identified Risks</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Risk Description</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {risks.map((risk, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{risk}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
            </div>
          )}

          {generatedPackage.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Generated Closeout Package Items</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Item/Clause</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {generatedPackage.map((item, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{item}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
              <Button className="mt-4" onClick={() => alert('Stub: Email closeout package')}>One-Click Email Closeout Package</Button>
            </div>
          )}

          <Button className="mt-6" variant="outline" onClick={() => router.push('/phase1b')}>Back to Submittals Wizard</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}
Updated Phase 1B Change Order: apps_web_app_phase1b_change-order_page.tsx

typescriptreact'use client'; // Client component for hooks and interactivity

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useSupabase } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from '@/components/ui/table';
import UploadZone from '@/components/forms/upload-zone';
import { parseFiles } from '@/lib/ai-parse'; // Stub for risks
import { generateFromRisks } from '@/lib/ai-generate'; // Enhanced for clauses
import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';

export default function ChangeOrder() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(null);
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [risks, setRisks] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [generatedClauses, setGeneratedClauses] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = useSupabase();

  useEffect(() => {
    const getSession = async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      setSession(session);
      if (!session) router.push('/');
    };
    getSession();

    const { data: authListener } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => setSession(session));
    return () => authListener.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, [router, supabase]);

  const handleUpload = async (acceptedFiles: File[]) => {
    setFiles(acceptedFiles);
    setUploading(true);
    setError(null);
    setRisks([]);
    setGeneratedClauses([]);

    try {
      // Standardized bucket: 'enki-storage' with phase sub-paths (e.g., 'jobs/' for setup, 'submittals/' for reviews).
      // RLS enforces user_${id}/ prefix for security—errors indicate auth/policy issues.
      const uploadedFiles = [];
      for (const file of acceptedFiles) {
        // Sanitize file name to remove invalid characters (e.g., brackets, spaces to underscores)
        const safeName = file.name.replace(/[\[\]]/g, '').replace(/\s/g, '_');
        const { data, error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('enki-storage')
          .upload(`submittals/user_${session?.user.id}/${safeName}`, file);
        if (uploadError) throw uploadError;
        uploadedFiles.push(safeName); // Track for logging
      }
      console.log('Upload successful:', uploadedFiles); // Log success with file names

      // Enhanced: Stub AI parse for risks with focus
      const parsedRisks = parseFiles(acceptedFiles, { focus: 'change-order' });
      console.log('Parsed risks:', parsedRisks); // Log risks for debug
      setRisks(parsedRisks);

      // Enhanced: Stub generate clauses with type and context
      const clauses = await generateFromRisks(parsedRisks, { type: 'clauses', context: { materialType: 'membrane' } });
      console.log('Generated clauses:', clauses); // Log for debug
      setGeneratedClauses(clauses);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Upload failed:', (err as Error).message);
      setError('Upload or processing failed: ' + (err as Error).message || "Upload failed - check RLS policies (e.g., authenticated role, user path match) or re-login.");
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!session) return null;

  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto p-4">
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Phase 1B: Change Order Review</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <p className="mb-4">Upload PDFs (change directives, RFIs) for AI review. Focus: Scope risks, pricing protection, sequencing impacts for waterproofing.</p>
          <UploadZone onUpload={handleUpload} />

          {uploading && <p className="mt-4">Uploading and processing...</p>}

          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive" className="mt-4">
              <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
              <AlertTitle>Error</AlertTitle>
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}

          {risks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Identified Risks</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Risk Description</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {risks.map((risk, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{risk}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
            </div>
          )}

          {generatedClauses.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Generated Change Order Clauses</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Clause</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {generatedClauses.map((clause, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{clause}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
              <Button className="mt-4" onClick={() => alert('Stub: Email protected change order')}>One-Click Email Change Order</Button>
            </div>
          )}

          <Button className="mt-6" variant="outline" onClick={() => router.push('/phase1b')}>Back to Submittals Wizard</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'; // For router.push
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { generateFromRisks } from '@/lib/ai-generate';

export default function Phase1B() {
  const [generatedExhibits, setGeneratedExhibits] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const router = useRouter(); // For navigation

  const handleGenerateSubmittals = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      // Stub risks from prior parse (future: Pass from Phase 1A or Supabase)
      const mockRisks = [
        { fileName: 'specs.pdf', highlights: ['Substrate mismatch', 'Lead time >4wks'] },
      ];
      const exhibits = await generateFromRisks(mockRisks, { type: 'exhibits' }); // Enhanced stub
      setGeneratedExhibits(exhibits);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('AI Generate Error:', error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <main className="flex min-h-screen flex-col items-center justify-center p-24">
      <Card className="w-[450px]">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Phase 1B: Submittal Generation Wizard</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>Review parsed risks and generate compliance exhibits/submittals with one click.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-4">
          <p>Based on uploaded specs/subcontracts: Stub risks detected (e.g., substrate mismatch, lead time &gt;4wks).</p>
          <Button onClick={handleGenerateSubmittals} disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? 'Generating...' : 'Generate Protection Exhibit'}
          </Button>
          {generatedExhibits.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4 space-y-4">
              <h4 className="text-sm font-medium">Generated Exhibit Clauses:</h4>
              {generatedExhibits.map((exhibit, idx) => (
                <Alert key={idx} variant="default">
                  <AlertTitle>{exhibit.fileName}</AlertTitle>
                  <AlertDescription>
                    <ul className="list-disc pl-4">
                      {exhibit.protections.map((clause: string, cIdx: number) => (
                        <li key={cIdx}>{clause}</li>
                      ))}
                    </ul>
                  </AlertDescription>
                </Alert>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          <div className="mt-6">
            <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Navigate to Phase 1 Workflows</h3>
            <div className="flex flex-col space-y-2 mt-2"> {/* Vertical layout for clarity */}
              <Button onClick={() => router.push('/phase1a')}>Job Setup (1A)</Button>
              <Button onClick={() => alert('Coming Soon: Product DB/Spec Matching (1C)')}>Product DB/Spec Matching (1C - Pending)</Button>
              <Button onClick={() => alert('Coming Soon: Kickoff/Compliance (1D)')}>Kickoff/Compliance (1D - Pending)</Button>
              <Button onClick={() => alert('Coming Soon: Submittal Log (1E)')}>Submittal Log (1E - Pending)</Button>
              <Button onClick={() => alert('Coming Soon: Scheduling/Progress (1F)')}>Scheduling/Progress (1F - Pending)</Button>
              <Button onClick={() => router.push('/phase1b/change-order')}>Change Orders (1G)</Button>
              <Button onClick={() => router.push('/phase1b/procurement')}>Procurement (1H)</Button>
              <Button onClick={() => router.push('/phase1b/invoice')}>Invoice Review (1I)</Button>
              <Button onClick={() => alert('Coming Soon: Payment Apps/Billing (1J)')}>Payment Apps/Billing (1J - Pending)</Button>
              <Button onClick={() => router.push('/phase1b/closeout')}>Closeout (1K)</Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </main>
  );
}

        Don't show againThe "Generate Protection Exhibit" button is present in the Phase 1B Submittal Generation Wizard (at /phase1b) as a core feature of this hub page, per the Master Blueprint's partial implementation of 1B. It serves as a quick stub to demonstrate the AI-assisted generation pattern (parse risks → generate compliance exhibits/submittals), using mock data from prior uploads (e.g., substrate mismatch or lead time risks from specs/subcontracts). This aligns with Enki's Phase 1 focus on office PM efficiency: reviewing parsed risks and creating protective clauses with one click, before navigating to specific workflows like procurement or invoice review. It's not redundant—it's the "wizard" entry point for submittal-related tasks, helping enforce discipline in risk reduction (e.g., GC confirmation on unpriced extras). If it feels misplaced, we can relocate it to a dedicated section or make it conditional (e.g., after uploads), but for now, it's intentional to showcase the generate/review pattern in the hub.
Regarding the console error ("TypeError: risk.split is not a function" when clicking the button): This is a bug due to a type mismatch in the stub data. The mockRisks are defined as an array of objects (with fileName and highlights), but the enhanced generateFromRisks function (from Step 4) expects an array of strings for risks. This causes the split call to fail on an object. To fix, we'll update the hub's handleGenerateSubmittals to use string risks (consistent with other pages) and adjust the display JSX to handle string outputs (instead of object properties like fileName and protections). This keeps the waterproofing focus (e.g., generating clauses with context for VOC or sequencing) without breaking patterns.
Here's the full updated code for apps_web_app_phase1b_page.tsx (copy-paste to replace the existing file). No other changes are needed—the error should resolve, and generation will produce string-based exhibits (e.g., "Exhibit Clause: Require GC/vendor confirmation on Substrate mismatch to mitigate liability.").
typescriptreact'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'; // For router.push
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { generateFromRisks } from '@/lib/ai-generate';

export default function Phase1B() {
  const [generatedExhibits, setGeneratedExhibits] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const router = useRouter(); // For navigation

  const handleGenerateSubmittals = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      // Stub risks as strings (future: Pass from Phase 1A or Supabase parse)
      const mockRisks = [
        "Risk: Substrate mismatch (e.g., incompatible membrane on gypsum)",
        "Risk: Lead time >4wks for flashing materials"
      ];
      const exhibits = await generateFromRisks(mockRisks, { type: 'exhibits', context: { jurisdiction: 'CA' } }); // Enhanced stub with context
      setGeneratedExhibits(exhibits);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('AI Generate Error:', error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <main className="flex min-h-screen flex-col items-center justify-center p-24">
      <Card className="w-[450px]">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Phase 1B: Submittal Generation Wizard</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>Review parsed risks and generate compliance exhibits/submittals with one click.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-4">
          <p>Based on uploaded specs/subcontracts: Stub risks detected (e.g., substrate mismatch, lead time &gt;4wks).</p>
          <Button onClick={handleGenerateSubmittals} disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? 'Generating...' : 'Generate Protection Exhibit'}
          </Button>
          {generatedExhibits.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4 space-y-4">
              <h4 className="text-sm font-medium">Generated Exhibit Clauses:</h4>
              {generatedExhibits.map((exhibit, idx) => (
                <Alert key={idx} variant="default">
                  <AlertDescription>{exhibit}</AlertDescription>
                </Alert>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          <div className="mt-6">
            <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Navigate to Phase 1 Workflows</h3>
            <div className="flex flex-col space-y-2 mt-2"> {/* Vertical layout for clarity */}
              <Button onClick={() => router.push('/phase1a')}>Job Setup (1A)</Button>
              <Button onClick={() => alert('Coming Soon: Product DB/Spec Matching (1C)')}>Product DB/Spec Matching (1C - Pending)</Button>
              <Button onClick={() => alert('Coming Soon: Kickoff/Compliance (1D)')}>Kickoff/Compliance (1D - Pending)</Button>
              <Button onClick={() => alert('Coming Soon: Submittal Log (1E)')}>Submittal Log (1E - Pending)</Button>
              <Button onClick={() => alert('Coming Soon: Scheduling/Progress (1F)')}>Scheduling/Progress (1F - Pending)</Button>
              <Button onClick={() => router.push('/phase1b/change-order')}>Change Orders (1G)</Button>
              <Button onClick={() => router.push('/phase1b/procurement')}>Procurement (1H)</Button>
              <Button onClick={() => router.push('/phase1b/invoice')}>Invoice Review (1I)</Button>
              <Button onClick={() => alert('Coming Soon: Payment Apps/Billing (1J)')}>Payment Apps/Billing (1J - Pending)</Button>
              <Button onClick={() => router.push('/phase1b/closeout')}>Closeout (1K)</Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </main>
  );
}

Updated Phase 1B (Repurposed to Submittal Log/Review): apps_web_app_phase1b_page.tsx
This becomes a dedicated workflow page like invoice, with upload/parse/generate for submittal risks (e.g., warranty flags, compliance exhibits). Removed the old generation button (now dashboard-navigated); added upload zone for consistency.
typescriptreact'use client'; // Client-side for hooks/interactivity

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useSupabase } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from '@/components/ui/table';
import UploadZone from '@/components/forms/upload-zone';
import { parseFiles } from '@/lib/ai-parse';
import { generateFromRisks } from '@/lib/ai-generate';
import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';

export default function Submittals() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(null);
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [risks, setRisks] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [generatedClauses, setGeneratedClauses] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = useSupabase();

  useEffect(() => {
    const getSession = async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      setSession(session);
      if (!session) router.push('/'); // Redirect guests
    };
    getSession();

    const { data: authListener } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => setSession(session));
    return () => authListener.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, [router, supabase]);

  const handleUpload = async (acceptedFiles: File[]) => {
    setFiles(acceptedFiles);
    setUploading(true);
    setError(null);
    setRisks([]);
    setGeneratedClauses([]);

    try {
      // Standardized bucket: 'enki-storage' with phase sub-paths (e.g., 'jobs/' for setup, 'submittals/' for reviews).
      // RLS enforces user_${id}/ prefix for security—errors indicate auth/policy issues.
      for (const file of acceptedFiles) {
        // Sanitize file name to remove invalid characters (e.g., brackets, spaces to underscores)
        const safeName = file.name.replace(/[\[\]]/g, '').replace(/\s/g, '_');
        const { error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('enki-storage')
          .upload(`submittals/user_${session?.user.id}/${safeName}`, file);
        if (uploadError) throw uploadError;
      }

      // Enhanced: Stub AI parse for risks with focus
      const parsedRisks = parseFiles(acceptedFiles, { focus: 'submittals' }); // Custom focus for warranty/compliance risks
      setRisks(parsedRisks);

      // Enhanced: Stub generate clauses/notes with type and context
      const clauses = await generateFromRisks(parsedRisks, { type: 'clauses', context: { jurisdiction: 'CA', materialType: 'membrane' } });
      setGeneratedClauses(clauses);
    } catch (err) {
      setError('Upload or processing failed: ' + (err as Error).message || "Upload failed - check RLS policies (e.g., authenticated role, user path match) or re-login.");
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!session) return null; // Loading during redirect

  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto p-4">
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Phase 1B: Submittals Review</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <p className="mb-4">Upload PDFs (vendor submittals, data sheets) for AI review. Focus: Warranty flags, compliance checks, substrate compatibilities for waterproofing.</p>
          <UploadZone onUpload={handleUpload} /> {/* Drag-drop upload here */}

          {uploading && <p className="mt-4">Uploading and processing...</p>}

          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive" className="mt-4">
              <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
              <AlertTitle>Error</AlertTitle>
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}

          {risks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Identified Risks</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Risk Description</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {risks.map((risk, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{risk}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
            </div>
          )}

          {generatedClauses.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Generated Submittal Clauses/Notes</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Clause/Note</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {generatedClauses.map((clause, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{clause}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
              <Button className="mt-4" onClick={() => alert('Stub: Email submittal review')}>One-Click Email Review</Button>
            </div>
          )}

          <Button className="mt-6" variant="outline" onClick={() => router.push('/dashboard')}>Back to Dashboard</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

New Submittal Log/Review Page (apps_web_app_submittals_page.tsx)
Create this file at app/submittals/page.tsx (Next.js app router convention). It's a stub with the full pattern for 1E: upload vendor submittals → parse for warranty/compliance risks → generate notes → display/email stub, with back to dashboard.
typescriptreact'use client'; // Client component for hooks and interactivity

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useSupabase } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from '@/components/ui/table';
import UploadZone from '@/components/forms/upload-zone';
import { parseFiles } from '@/lib/ai-parse';
import { generateFromRisks } from '@/lib/ai-generate';
import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';

export default function SubmittalsReview() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(null);
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [risks, setRisks] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [generatedNotes, setGeneratedNotes] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = useSupabase();

  useEffect(() => {
    const getSession = async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      setSession(session);
      if (!session) router.push('/');
    };
    getSession();

    const { data: authListener } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => setSession(session));
    return () => authListener.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, [router, supabase]);

  const handleUpload = async (acceptedFiles: File[]) => {
    setFiles(acceptedFiles);
    setUploading(true);
    setError(null);
    setRisks([]);
    setGeneratedNotes([]);

    try {
      // Standardized bucket: 'enki-storage' with phase sub-paths (e.g., 'jobs/' for setup, 'submittals/' for reviews).
      // RLS enforces user_${id}/ prefix for security—errors indicate auth/policy issues.
      const uploadedFiles = [];
      for (const file of acceptedFiles) {
        const safeName = file.name.replace(/[\[\]]/g, '').replace(/\s/g, '_');
        const { data, error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('enki-storage')
          .upload(`submittals/user_${session?.user.id}/${safeName}`, file);
        if (uploadError) throw uploadError;
        uploadedFiles.push(safeName);
      }
      console.log('Upload successful:', uploadedFiles);

      const parsedRisks = parseFiles(acceptedFiles, { focus: 'submittals' }); // Custom for warranty/compliance
      console.log('Parsed risks:', parsedRisks);
      setRisks(parsedRisks);

      const notes = await generateFromRisks(parsedRisks, { type: 'notes', context: { jurisdiction: 'CA', materialType: 'membrane' } });
      console.log('Generated notes:', notes);
      setGeneratedNotes(notes);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Upload failed:', (err as Error).message);
      setError('Upload or processing failed: ' + (err as Error).message || "Upload failed - check RLS policies or re-login.");
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!session) return null;

  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto p-4">
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Phase 1E: Submittal Log/Review Hub</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <p className="mb-4">Upload PDFs (vendor submittals, data sheets) for AI review. Focus: Warranty flags, compliance checks, substrate compatibilities for waterproofing (stubbed for Phase 1).</p>
          <UploadZone onUpload={handleUpload} />

          {uploading && <p className="mt-4">Uploading and processing...</p>}

          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive" className="mt-4">
              <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
              <AlertTitle>Error</AlertTitle>
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}

          {risks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Identified Risks</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Risk Description</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {risks.map((risk, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{risk}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
            </div>
          )}

          {generatedNotes.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Generated Review Notes</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Note/Clause</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {generatedNotes.map((note, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{note}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
              <Button className="mt-4" onClick={() => alert('Stub: Email submittal review notes')}>One-Click Email Review</Button>
            </div>
          )}

          <Button className="mt-6" variant="outline" onClick={() => router.push('/dashboard')}>Back to Dashboard</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

  };

  if (!session) return null;

  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto p-4">

          <CardTitle>Phase 1E: Submittal Log/Review Hub</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <p className="mb-4">Upload PDFs (vendor submittals, data sheets) for AI review. Focus: Warranty flags, compliance checks, substrate compatibilities for waterproofing (stubbed for Phase 1).</p>
          <UploadZone onUpload={handleUpload} />

          {uploading && <p className="mt-4">Uploading and processing...</p>}

          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive" className="mt-4">
              <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
              <AlertTitle>Error</AlertTitle>
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}

          {risks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Identified Risks</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Risk Description</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {risks.map((risk, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{risk}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
            </div>
          )}

          {generatedNotes.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Generated Review Notes</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Note/Clause</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {generatedNotes.map((note, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{note}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
              <Button className="mt-4" onClick={() => alert('Stub: Email submittal review notes')}>One-Click Email Review</Button>
            </div>
          )}

          <Button className="mt-6" variant="outline" onClick={() => router.push('/dashboard')}>Back to Dashboard</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}The button on the dashboard for submittals is not correct. It point to /submittals, when it should point to submittals/page.tsxThe button link in the dashboard code is actually correct as-is for Next.js app router conventions: router.push('/submittals') navigates to the route /submittals, which is served by the file at app/submittals/page.tsx (assuming that's where you placed it). The URL path (/submittals) doesn't include "/page.tsx"—that's just the file structure. If you're seeing an error or 404, it could be due to file placement (e.g., ensure it's under app/submittals/page.tsx, not a sub-folder like app/phase1b/submittals/page.tsx), or a dev server restart needed after creating the file.
If you meant to have a different route (e.g., /phase1e/submittals for better organization under Phase 1E), we can adjust—let me know. Update Pages with Toasts and Logs: In handleUpload (all Phase 1A/1B pages and submittals), add toasts for success/errors, expand logs for debug (e.g., risk details, generation context). Example for Phase 1A (apply pattern to others: import toast, use in try/catch/finally).typescriptreact'use client';

import { useState } from "react";
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import UploadZone from "@/components/forms/upload-zone";
import { parseFiles } from "@/lib/ai-parse";
import { generateFromRisks } from "@/lib/ai-generate";
import { useSupabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import toast from "react-hot-toast"; // NEW: For user-facing notifications

export default function Phase1A() {
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [risks, setRisks] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [exhibits, setExhibits] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const supabase = useSupabase();

  const handleUpload = async (uploadedFiles: File[]) => {
    setFiles(uploadedFiles);
    setError(null);

    let parsedRisks: string[] = [];
    try {
      // Check session and role
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      if (!session) throw new Error("No session - sign in required.");
      const { data: user } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      console.log('User role:', user.user?.role); // Debug: Role check

      if (user.user?.role !== 'authenticated') throw new Error("Role not authenticated - re-login or check Supabase auth.");

      // Upload with expanded logs
      const uploadPromises = uploadedFiles.map(async (file) => {
        const safeName = file.name.replace(/[$$  $$]/g, '').replace(/\s/g, '_');
        const { data, error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('enki-storage')
          .upload(`jobs/user_${session?.user.id}/${safeName}`, file, { upsert: true });

        if (uploadError) {
          console.error('Upload error for file', file.name, ':', uploadError); // Enhanced log
          throw uploadError;
        }
        console.log('Uploaded file:', data.path); // Debug: Path confirmation
        return data;
      });

      await Promise.all(uploadPromises);
      console.log('All uploads successful'); // Debug
      toast.success('Upload successful!'); // NEW: User toast
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error("Upload/Parse Error details:", err); // Enhanced log
      setError(err.message || "Upload failed - check RLS policies (e.g., authenticated role, user path match) or re-login. Stubbing parse.");
      toast.error(setError || 'Upload failed - try re-login.'); // NEW: Error toast
    } finally {
      parsedRisks = parseFiles(uploadedFiles, { focus: 'setup' });
      console.log('Parsed risks:', parsedRisks); // Debug
      setRisks(parsedRisks);
      const generatedExhibits = await generateFromRisks(parsedRisks, { type: 'exhibits', context: { jurisdiction: 'CA' } });
      console.log('Generated exhibits:', generatedExhibits); // Debug
      setExhibits(generatedExhibits);
      if (generatedExhibits.length > 0) toast.success('Exhibits generated!'); // NEW: Success toast
    }
  };

  // Return JSX unchanged...
}

Here's the full updated code for Phase 1A as an example (copy-paste to replace apps_web_app_phase1a_page.tsx). Apply the same pattern to the Phase 1B sub-pages (replace their handleUpload with similar toasts/logs).
typescriptreact'use client';

import { useState } from "react";
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import UploadZone from "@/components/forms/upload-zone";
import { parseFiles } from "@/lib/ai-parse";
import { generateFromRisks } from "@/lib/ai-generate";
import { useSupabase } from "@/lib/supabase";
import toast from "react-hot-toast"; // NEW: For user-facing notifications

export default function Phase1A() {
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [risks, setRisks] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [exhibits, setExhibits] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const supabase = useSupabase();

  const handleUpload = async (uploadedFiles: File[]) => {
    setFiles(uploadedFiles);
    setError(null);

    let parsedRisks: string[] = [];
    try {
      // Check session and role
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      if (!session) throw new Error("No session - sign in required.");
      const { data: user } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      console.log('User role:', user.user?.role); // Debug: Role check

      if (user.user?.role !== 'authenticated') throw new Error("Role not authenticated - re-login or check Supabase auth.");

      // Upload with expanded logs
      const uploadPromises = uploadedFiles.map(async (file) => {
        const safeName = file.name.replace(/[\[\]]/g, '').replace(/\s/g, '_');
        const { data, error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('enki-storage')
          .upload(`jobs/user_${session?.user.id}/${safeName}`, file, { upsert: true });

        if (uploadError) {
          console.error('Upload error for file', file.name, ':', uploadError); // Enhanced log
          throw uploadError;
        }
        console.log('Uploaded file:', data.path); // Debug: Path confirmation
        return data;
      });

      await Promise.all(uploadPromises);
      console.log('All uploads successful'); // Debug
      toast.success('Upload successful!'); // NEW: Success toast for user
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error("Upload/Parse Error details:", err); // Enhanced log
      const errorMsg = err.message || "Upload failed - check RLS policies (e.g., authenticated role, user path match) or re-login. Stubbing parse.";
      setError(errorMsg);
      toast.error(errorMsg); // NEW: Error toast for user
    } finally {
      parsedRisks = parseFiles(uploadedFiles, { focus: 'setup' });
      console.log('Parsed risks:', parsedRisks); // Debug: Risks output
      setRisks(parsedRisks);
      const generatedExhibits = await generateFromRisks(parsedRisks, { type: 'exhibits', context: { jurisdiction: 'CA' } });
      console.log('Generated exhibits:', generatedExhibits); // Debug: Generated output
      setExhibits(generatedExhibits);
      if (generatedExhibits.length > 0) toast.success('Exhibits generated successfully!'); // NEW: Success toast for generation
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col items-center justify-center p-24">
      <Card className="w-[600px]">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Job Setup Wizard</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>Upload specs/subcontracts PDFs for risk parsing and exhibit generation.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-4">
          <UploadZone onUpload={handleUpload} />
          {files.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Uploaded Files:</h3>
              <ul className="list-disc pl-5">
                {files.map((file, idx) => (
                  <li key={idx}>{file.name}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}
          {risks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Detected Risks:</h3>
              {risks.map((risk, idx) => (
                <Alert key={idx} variant="warning">
                  <AlertDescription>{risk}</AlertDescription>
                </Alert>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          {exhibits.length > 0 && (
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="font-semibold">Generated Exhibits:</h3>
              {exhibits.map((exhibit, idx) => (
                <Alert key={idx} variant="default">
                  <AlertDescription>{exhibit}</AlertDescription>
                </Alert>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive">
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}
          <Button onClick={() => setFiles([])} variant="outline">Clear Files</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

Below is the complete, up-to-date code for all Phase 1B pages based on the current Enki implementation. These follow the core patterns: drag-drop upload via UploadZone → secure storage in 'enki-storage' with RLS/user paths → parseFiles for waterproofing-specific risks (e.g., lead times, rate mismatches, warranty incompleteness) → generateFromRisks for clauses/notes with context (e.g., jurisdiction for VOC) → display in Tables/Alerts → one-click email stubs. 1. Hub Page: apps_web_app_phase1b_page.tsx (Submittals Wizard)
This is the entry wizard for stubbed risk generation (no upload—uses mocks for quick exhibits).
typescriptreact'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'; // For router.push
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { generateFromRisks } from '@/lib/ai-generate';
import toast from "react-hot-toast"; // For notifications

export default function Phase1B() {
  const [generatedExhibits, setGeneratedExhibits] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const router = useRouter();

  const handleGenerateSubmittals = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      // Stub risks as strings (future: Integrate from uploads or Supabase)
      const mockRisks = [
        "Risk: Substrate mismatch (e.g., incompatible membrane on gypsum)",
        "Risk: Lead time >4wks for flashing materials"
      ];
      console.log('Generating from mock risks:', mockRisks); // Debug log
      const exhibits = await generateFromRisks(mockRisks, { type: 'exhibits', context: { jurisdiction: 'CA' } });
      console.log('Generated exhibits:', exhibits); // Debug log
      setGeneratedExhibits(exhibits);
      toast.success('Exhibits generated successfully!');
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('AI Generate Error:', error);
      toast.error('Generation failed - check console for details.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <main className="flex min-h-screen flex-col items-center justify-center p-24">
      <Card className="w-[450px]">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Phase 1B: Submittal Generation Wizard</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>Review parsed risks and generate compliance exhibits/submittals with one click.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-4">
          <p>Based on uploaded specs/subcontracts: Stub risks detected (e.g., substrate mismatch, lead time &gt;4wks).</p>
          <Button onClick={handleGenerateSubmittals} disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? 'Generating...' : 'Generate Protection Exhibit'}
          </Button>
          {generatedExhibits.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4 space-y-4">
              <h4 className="text-sm font-medium">Generated Exhibit Clauses:</h4>
              {generatedExhibits.map((exhibit, idx) => (
                <Alert key={idx} variant="default">
                  <AlertDescription>{exhibit}</AlertDescription>
                </Alert>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          <Button className="mt-6" variant="outline" onClick={() => router.push('/dashboard')}>Back to Dashboard</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </main>
  );
}
2. Procurement: apps_web_app_phase1b_procurement_page.tsx
Focus: Stock/lead time risks for materials.
typescriptreact'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useSupabase } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from '@/components/ui/table';
import UploadZone from '@/components/forms/upload-zone';
import { parseFiles } from '@/lib/ai-parse';
import { generateFromRisks } from '@/lib/ai-generate';
import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import toast from "react-hot-toast"; // NEW: For notifications

export default function Procurement() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(null);
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [risks, setRisks] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [generatedClauses, setGeneratedClauses] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = useSupabase();

  useEffect(() => {
    const getSession = async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      setSession(session);
      if (!session) router.push('/'); // Redirect guests
    };
    getSession();

    const { data: authListener } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => setSession(session));
    return () => authListener.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, [router, supabase]);

  const handleUpload = async (acceptedFiles: File[]) => {
    setFiles(acceptedFiles);
    setUploading(true);
    setError(null);
    setRisks([]);
    setGeneratedClauses([]);

    try {
      // Standardized bucket: 'enki-storage' with phase sub-paths (e.g., 'jobs/' for setup, 'submittals/' for reviews).
      // RLS enforces user_${id}/ prefix for security—errors indicate auth/policy issues.
      for (const file of acceptedFiles) {
        const safeName = file.name.replace(/[\[\]]/g, '').replace(/\s/g, '_');
        const { error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('enki-storage')
          .upload(`submittals/user_${session?.user.id}/${safeName}`, file);
        if (uploadError) throw uploadError;
      }
      console.log('All uploads successful'); // Debug
      toast.success('Upload successful!');

      const parsedRisks = parseFiles(acceptedFiles, { focus: 'procurement' });
      console.log('Parsed risks:', parsedRisks); // Debug
      setRisks(parsedRisks);

      const clauses = await generateFromRisks(parsedRisks, { type: 'clauses', context: { materialType: 'flashing' } });
      console.log('Generated clauses:', clauses); // Debug
      setGeneratedClauses(clauses);
      toast.success('Clauses generated!');
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Upload/Parse Error details:', err); // Enhanced log
      const errorMsg = 'Upload or processing failed: ' + (err as Error).message || "Upload failed - check RLS policies or re-login.";
      setError(errorMsg);
      toast.error(errorMsg);
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!session) return null; // Loading during redirect

  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto p-4">
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Phase 1B: Procurement Review</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <p className="mb-4">Upload PDFs (quotes, vendor data, specs) for AI review. Focus: Stock issues, lead times, rate consistency for waterproofing materials.</p>
          <UploadZone onUpload={handleUpload} />

          {uploading && <p className="mt-4">Uploading and processing...</p>}

          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive" className="mt-4">
              <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
              <AlertTitle>Error</AlertTitle>
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}

          {risks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Identified Risks</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Risk Description</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {risks.map((risk, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{risk}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
            </div>
          )}

          {generatedClauses.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Generated PO Clauses</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Clause</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {generatedClauses.map((clause, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{clause}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
              <Button className="mt-4" onClick={() => alert('Stub: Email protected POs')}>One-Click Email POs</Button>
            </div>
          )}

          <Button className="mt-6" variant="outline" onClick={() => router.push('/dashboard')}>Back to Dashboard</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

    const getSession = async () => {

    return () => authListener.subscription.unsubscribe();

    setFiles(acceptedFiles);
    setUploading(true);
    setError(null);
    setRisks([]);
    setGeneratedNotes([]);

    try {
      // Standardized bucket: 'enki-storage' with phase sub-paths (e.g., 'jobs/' for setup, 'submittals/' for reviews).
      // RLS enforces user_${id}/ prefix for security—errors indicate auth/policy issues.
      const uploadedFiles = [];

        const safeName = file.name.replace(/[\[\]]/g, '').replace(/\s/g, '_');

          .from('enki-storage')

        if (uploadError) throw uploadError;
        uploadedFiles.push(safeName);

      console.log('Parsed risks:', parsedRisks); // Debug

      const notes = await generateFromRisks(parsedRisks, { type: 'notes', context: { jurisdiction: 'CA' } });
      console.log('Generated notes:', notes); // Debug

      toast.success('Notes generated!');

            <Alert variant="destructive" className="mt-4">
              <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4" />

                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Risk Description</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {risks.map((risk, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{risk}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
            </div>
          )}

      console.log('Generated package:', packageItems); // Debug
      setGeneratedPackage(packageItems);
      toast.success('Package items generated!');
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Upload/Parse Error details:', err); // Enhanced log

              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Risk Description</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>

                  {risks.map((risk, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{risk}</TableCell>

                  ))}
                </TableBody>

For the submittals page (app/phase1b/submittals/page.tsx), use the same pattern—add import toast from "react-hot-toast";, then insert toasts and logs in handleUpload as above (success for upload/generate, error in catch, logs for paths/risks/notes). This completes Step 6 across all.
Once updated, test (toasts on upload success/failure, logs in console), and we'll advance to Step 7: Prep for 1C.Shouldn't apps_web_app_phase1b_page.tsx be blank right now with everything moved to submittals/page.tsx?No, apps_web_app_phase1b_page.tsx shouldn't be blank or removed—it's intentional to keep it as the dedicated "Submittals Generation Wizard" entry point for Phase 1B's partial implementation (per the Master Blueprint's focus on AI-assisted exhibit/clause generation for compliance and profit protection, e.g., requiring GC confirmation on sequencing dependencies or VOC limits in submittals). Moving everything to /phase1b/submittals/page.tsx (the 1E log/review hub) would merge two distinct workflows, potentially confusing PM users who need quick generation vs. full review/logging.
Why Keep /phase1b Separate?

Blueprint Alignment: Phase 1B is the "Submittals Wizard" for rapid parsing/generation (stubbed with mocks for risks like substrate incompatibilities or lead times >4wks, outputting protective clauses with one click). It's not the full 1E "Submittal Log/Review Hub," which is for ongoing tracking (e.g., warranty flags, vendor data uploads, compliance billing reviews). Separating them enforces discipline: wizard for initial setup/generation, hub for iterative review.
Pattern Reuse: Both reuse drag-drop → parse → generate, but /phase1b is mock-based (no upload in current stub, to simulate quick exhibits), while /phase1b/submittals is upload-focused (for real vendor PDFs, parsing for warranty-specific risks like expiration dates tied to material types).
Navigation Flow: Dashboard links to /phase1b for wizard (partial 1B), and /phase1b/submittals for log (1E). This scales for pending features (e.g., integrate 1F Gantt diffs into log for sequencing risks).
User Experience: PMs can hit /phase1b for fast "Generate Protection Exhibit" (e.g., CA-jurisdiction-aware clauses for VOC), then navigate to /phase1b/submittals for detailed review—avoiding overload in one page.

Suggested Adjustments (If Needed)
If the wizard feels underutilized in its current stub form, we can enhance it slightly (e.g., add optional upload for dynamic risks, or tie to Supabase for stored job data from 1A)—but don't blank it. For example, update the code to include a stub upload if you want consistency:
typescriptreact// Example enhancement to /phase1b/page.tsx (optional - copy if desired; otherwise keep original)
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import UploadZone from '@/components/forms/upload-zone'; // Optional: Add for dynamic upload
import { parseFiles } from '@/lib/ai-parse';
import { generateFromRisks } from '@/lib/ai-generate';
import toast from "react-hot-toast";

export default function Phase1B() {
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [generatedExhibits, setGeneratedExhibits] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const router = useRouter();

  const handleGenerateSubmittals = async (uploadedFiles: File[] = []) => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      let mockRisks = [
        "Risk: Substrate mismatch (e.g., incompatible membrane on gypsum)",
        "Risk: Lead time >4wks for flashing materials"
      ];
      if (uploadedFiles.length > 0) {
        mockRisks = parseFiles(uploadedFiles, { focus: 'submittals' });
        console.log('Parsed risks from upload:', mockRisks);
      }
      const exhibits = await generateFromRisks(mockRisks, { type: 'exhibits', context: { jurisdiction: 'CA' } });
      console.log('Generated exhibits:', exhibits);
      setGeneratedExhibits(exhibits);
      toast.success('Exhibits generated!');
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('AI Generate Error:', error);
      toast.error('Generation failed.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <main className="flex min-h-screen flex-col items-center justify-center p-24">
      <Card className="w-[450px]">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Phase 1B: Submittal Generation Wizard</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>Upload optional PDFs or use stubs to generate compliance exhibits/submittals.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-4">
          <UploadZone onUpload={handleGenerateSubmittals} /> {/* Optional upload for dynamic risks */}
          <Button onClick={() => handleGenerateSubmittals()} disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? 'Generating...' : 'Generate Protection Exhibit (from stubs)'}
          </Button>
          {generatedExhibits.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4 space-y-4">
              <h4 className="text-sm font-medium">Generated Exhibit Clauses:</h4>
              {generatedExhibits.map((exhibit, idx) => (
                <Alert key={idx} variant="default">
                  <AlertDescription>{exhibit}</AlertDescription>
                </Alert>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          <Button className="mt-6" variant="outline" onClick={() => router.push('/dashboard')}>Back to Dashboard</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </main>
  );
}
This optional enhancement adds upload to the wizard for flexibility (e.g., parse real submittals for VOC risks), but falls back to mocks—use if it fits; otherwise, stick to your original blank-generation version. Either way, the page remains relevant for Phase 1B's AI assist pattern.

Let me know!please show me the full code for submittals/page.tsxtypescriptreact'use client'; // Client component for hooks and interactivity

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useSupabase } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from '@/components/ui/table';
import UploadZone from '@/components/forms/upload-zone';
import { parseFiles } from '@/lib/ai-parse';
import { generateFromRisks } from '@/lib/ai-generate';
import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import toast from "react-hot-toast";

export default function SubmittalsReview() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(null);
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [risks, setRisks] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [generatedNotes, setGeneratedNotes] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = useSupabase();

  useEffect(() => {
    const getSession = async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      setSession(session);
      if (!session) router.push('/');
    };
    getSession();

    const { data: authListener } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => setSession(session));
    return () => authListener.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, [router, supabase]);

  const handleUpload = async (acceptedFiles: File[]) => {
    setFiles(acceptedFiles);
    setUploading(true);
    setError(null);
    setRisks([]);
    setGeneratedNotes([]);

    try {
      // Standardized bucket: 'enki-storage' with phase sub-paths (e.g., 'jobs/' for setup, 'submittals/' for reviews).
      // RLS enforces user_${id}/ prefix for security—errors indicate auth/policy issues.
      const uploadedFiles = [];
      for (const file of acceptedFiles) {
        const safeName = file.name.replace(/[\[\]]/g, '').replace(/\s/g, '_');
        const { data, error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('enki-storage')
          .upload(`submittals/user_${session?.user.id}/${safeName}`, file);
        if (uploadError) throw uploadError;
        uploadedFiles.push(safeName);
      }
      console.log('Upload successful:', uploadedFiles); // Debug
      toast.success('Upload successful!');

      const parsedRisks = parseFiles(acceptedFiles, { focus: 'submittals' }); // Custom for warranty/compliance
      console.log('Parsed risks:', parsedRisks); // Debug
      setRisks(parsedRisks);

      const notes = await generateFromRisks(parsedRisks, { type: 'notes', context: { jurisdiction: 'CA', materialType: 'membrane' } });
      console.log('Generated notes:', notes); // Debug
      setGeneratedNotes(notes);
      toast.success('Notes generated!');
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Upload/Parse Error details:', err); // Enhanced log
      const errorMsg = 'Upload or processing failed: ' + (err as Error).message || "Upload failed - check RLS policies or re-login.";
      setError(errorMsg);
      toast.error(errorMsg);
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!session) return null;

  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto p-4">
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Phase 1E: Submittal Log/Review Hub</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <p className="mb-4">Upload PDFs (vendor submittals, data sheets) for AI review. Focus: Warranty flags, compliance checks, substrate compatibilities for waterproofing (stubbed for Phase 1).</p>
          <UploadZone onUpload={handleUpload} />

          {uploading && <p className="mt-4">Uploading and processing...</p>}

          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive" className="mt-4">
              <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
              <AlertTitle>Error</AlertTitle>
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}

          {risks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Identified Risks</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Risk Description</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {risks.map((risk, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{risk}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
            </div>
          )}

          {generatedNotes.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Generated Review Notes</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Note/Clause</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {generatedNotes.map((note, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{note}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
              <Button className="mt-4" onClick={() => alert('Stub: Email submittal review notes')}>One-Click Email Review</Button>
            </div>
          )}

          <Button className="mt-6" variant="outline" onClick={() => router.push('/dashboard')}>Back to Dashboard</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}All the toast changes have been made. Copy-paste this full code into the new file. It includes upload → parse (custom focus for PDS details) → generate (mock matching risks) → display with alerts/toasts.typescriptreact'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useSupabase } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from '@/components/ui/table';
import UploadZone from '@/components/forms/upload-zone';
import { parseFiles } from '@/lib/ai-parse';
import { generateFromRisks } from '@/lib/ai-generate';
import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import toast from "react-hot-toast";

export default function ProductDB() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(null);
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [risks, setRisks] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [generatedMatches, setGeneratedMatches] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = useSupabase();

  useEffect(() => {
    const getSession = async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      setSession(session);
      if (!session) router.push('/');
    };
    getSession();

    const { data: authListener } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => setSession(session));
    return () => authListener.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, [router, supabase]);

  const handleUpload = async (acceptedFiles: File[]) => {
    setFiles(acceptedFiles);
    setUploading(true);
    setError(null);
    setRisks([]);
    setGeneratedMatches([]);

    try {
      // Upload PDS PDFs to 'enki-storage/pds/user_{id}/'
      const uploadedFiles = [];
      for (const file of acceptedFiles) {
        const safeName = file.name.replace(/[$$  $$]/g, '').replace(/\s/g, '_');
        const { data, error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
          .from('enki-storage')
          .upload(`pds/user_${session?.user.id}/${safeName}`, file);
        if (uploadError) throw uploadError;
        uploadedFiles.push(safeName);
      }
      console.log('Upload successful:', uploadedFiles);
      toast.success('PDS upload successful!');

      // Stub parse for PDS details (e.g., VOC, lead times, substrates)
      const parsedDetails = parseFiles(acceptedFiles, { focus: 'pds' }); // Custom focus for product data
      console.log('Parsed PDS details:', parsedDetails);
      setRisks(parsedDetails); // Treat as 'details' for matching

      // Stub generate for spec matching/risks (future: Compare to job specs from 1A)
      const matches = await generateFromRisks(parsedDetails, { type: 'matches', context: { jurisdiction: 'CA', materialType: 'membrane' } }); // Mock risks like "Mismatch: VOC exceeds regs"
      console.log('Generated matches/risks:', matches);
      setGeneratedMatches(matches);
      toast.success('Spec matching complete!');
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Upload/Parse Error details:', err);
      const errorMsg = 'Upload or matching failed: ' + (err as Error).message || "Check RLS or re-login.";
      setError(errorMsg);
      toast.error(errorMsg);
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  };

  if (!session) return null;

  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto p-4">
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Phase 1C: Product DB/Spec Matching (Stub)</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <p className="mb-4">Upload vendor PDS PDFs (e.g., Tremco membranes) for AI parsing and mock matching against job specs. Focus: Flag risks like VOC limits, lead times, substrate compatibilities for waterproofing profit protection.</p>
          <UploadZone onUpload={handleUpload} />

          {uploading && <p className="mt-4">Uploading and matching...</p>}

          {error && (
            <Alert variant="destructive" className="mt-4">
              <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
              <AlertTitle>Error</AlertTitle>
              <AlertDescription>{error}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          )}

          {risks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Parsed PDS Details</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Detail</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {risks.map((detail, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{detail}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
            </div>
          )}

          {generatedMatches.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">Generated Spec Matches/Risks</h3>
              <Table>
                <TableHeader>
                  <TableRow>
                    <TableHead>Match/Risk</TableHead>
                  </TableRow>
                </TableHeader>
                <TableBody>
                  {generatedMatches.map((match, index) => (
                    <TableRow key={index}>
                      <TableCell>{match}</TableCell>
                    </TableRow>
                  ))}
                </TableBody>
              </Table>
              <Button className="mt-4" onClick={() => alert('Stub: Email spec matching report')}>One-Click Email Report</Button>
            </div>
          )}

          <Button className="mt-6" variant="outline" onClick={() => router.push('/dashboard')}>Back to Dashboard</Button>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}
